### CORRELATION GROUPS

- `2026_09_TB_CHI::SCORING_UP` (same, strong (qualitative)): game total over, either team total over, QB passing yards over, receiver yards/receptions over, anytime TD yes, total TDs over — These rise and fall together with game scoring and pace.
- `2026_09_TB_CHI::CHI_SIDE` (same, strong (qualitative)): CHI moneyline, CHI spread, CHI team total over, CHI player props over — Team-side markets share the same game-script risk.
- `2026_09_TB_CHI::GAME_SCRIPT_OPPOSED` (opposed, moderate (qualitative)): favourite rushing volume over, favourite passing volume over — A team that leads runs more and throws less; volume props on the same team can conflict.

### KEY QUESTIONS FOR THE HANDICAPPER

1. Zavier Scott (RB, CHI) is ruled out. Is the market's price on his replacement's usage already reflecting the redistributed role, or still anchored to a committee?
2. Jalen McMillan (WR, TB) is ruled out. Is the market's price on his replacement's usage already reflecting the redistributed role, or still anchored to a committee?
